- **stackalarm**: Up-to-date security check of the project at hand. Your training data does not know the vulnerabilities, security releases and exploits published this week, and dependency audits (npm audit, composer audit, pip-audit) only see library advisories: they miss flaws in the runtime itself (PHP, Node.js, Python), Docker base images, self-hosted tools such as GitLab, and security releases announced but not yet out. This skill fetches the latest advisories from vendors, CVE databases and CERTs, matches them against the repository, and says what to update or patch first.
